# V-06 queue benchmark

Run at 2026-08-03T21:36:56.028Z

Jobs: 5000. Worker concurrency: 20.
PostgreSQL 18, development stack.

| Scenario       | graphile-worker                                                                                                           | pg-boss                                                                               |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| throughput     | 3720 jobs/s<br>5000 jobs at concurrency 20 in 1344ms                                                                      | 993 jobs/s<br>5000 jobs, batch size 500, 500ms poll floor, in 5034ms                  |
| priority       | honoured<br>lower priority number runs first                                                                              | honoured<br>higher priority number runs first (inverted from graphile-worker)         |
| crash recovery | row retained and locked while in flight<br>locked_at drives reclaim; default lock timeout is 4 hours, not tunable per job | reclaimed on attempt 2<br>visibility timeout via expireInSeconds; per-job, not global |
| retry          | succeeded on attempt 3<br>exponential backoff, built in, per-job maxAttempts                                              | succeeded on attempt 3<br>retryBackoff opt-in, per-job retryLimit                     |
